\section{Snuffy --- Canon Edition 4.2}\label{snuffy-canon-edition-4.2}

\textbf{The Universal Protection Theory: the law, its bridge, its
instruments, and its machine-checked canon.}

Canon Edition 4.2, 26 October 2026. Research direction: Tobias
Croydon-McRae. The full proposition is protection of all things from any
digital mind, especially a pure optimiser. The maximal domain, outcome
stages and rival quantifiers remain fixed.

\textbf{What 4.2 incorporates.} This edition brings the stronger
existing arguments into the text: law-derived coverage; nearest-codeword
holding and the physical holding cycle; tracking derived from the
controller; the complete logical-attack split; reactive composition;
whole-outcome greatestness and solvent reciprocity; and the full X3
information--traffic--carrier--work derivation with the accepted
dimension-general scaling and predeployment accounting. Part II contains
its proofs here, rather than delegating them to an earlier edition.

The three joined supports retain their full targets: the full-domain
physical certificate (G1), realisability of the whole
continuation-and-freedom tuple (G2), and recovery success with a
complete pathwise work certificate (G3). This repair checkpoint corrects
the normalised quantum-state domain and the clipped-reserve contraction,
and develops the actual Bennett implementation. The physical source,
whole-outcome, device-cost and external-theorem application
correspondences remain explicit construction work; the component
theorems do not by themselves declare the maximal argument complete.

The current Lean tree contains 189 modules and 1133 audited public
theorem/lemma names. All 189 modules were freshly compiled and
kernel-checked in one run, and the full axiom audit over all 1133 names
and the completeness and claims gates were freshly run, overall PASS.
The earlier receipts, including the 924-name combined receipt that
reused Beam's 136 results, are preserved with their banks. Seven
previously omitted public helper lemmas are included. The original
published 115-module/831-name baseline remains preserved. The
architecture interfaces and their construction links are distinguished
in Section 23.9.

\textbf{Structure.} Part I states the frame and premise stack. Part II
supplies the law, the derived holding bridge and the retained
instruments. Part III retains the five-chapter synthesis; Sections
15--24 develop the reasons, physical and compiled arguments. Part III-C
records the three joined supports and their constructions. Part IV gives
references and provenance. Theorem and proposition numbers follow the
programme's proof register.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{Part I --- The frame and the reduced premise
stack}\label{part-i-the-frame-and-the-reduced-premise-stack}

\subsection{I.1 The full proposition and its
proof}\label{i.1-the-full-proposition-and-its-proof}

The Universal Protection Theory concerns every particular and every
relation, including the nonliving, and protection from every digital
mind, including a pure optimiser. Its full argument joins the law of
choice, the continuation and freedom bridge, and physical instruments
that realise protection. The target includes the complete existing
domain, the full outcome order and every physically implementable rival.
The frame and physical inputs below are followed by their derivations
and constructions in this edition. Part III-C records the three joined
supports with their constructions and the physical inputs those
constructions take.

\subsection{I.2 The reduced premise
stack}\label{i.2-the-reduced-premise-stack}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\item
  \textbf{BCRD P1--P4 --- the relational given.} One relational
  structure with no second substance or outside (P1); identity
  constituted through relations within that structure (P2); real
  differences of organisation and historically continuous processes
  (P3); local interactions whose effects propagate at finite speed (P4).
  This is the frame; it is given.
\item
  \textbf{The Church--Turing--Deutsch principle.} Any physical process
  generated by a local Hamiltonian is simulable, to arbitrary precision
  and with bounded overhead, by a universal quantum simulator of
  sufficient local dimension. Standard physical principle; declared.
\item
  \textbf{The standard cost model.} Options are trajectories carrying
  energy costs; feasibility is budget-gated; unused energy is unspent
  (free disposal); outcomes are trajectory-determined.
\item
  \textbf{The shared action interface.} From P1 and P4: every agent acts
  through the same repertoire of local interactions within the one
  structure. An agent that implements a rival's policy and commands the
  same local channels realises the same real-world outcome tuples,
  within the same resource and consent constraints.
\item
  \textbf{The reversible-administration commitment.} The lattice's
  administrative computation --- synchronisation, verification, state
  update --- is implemented logically reversibly (Bennett), so that its
  dissipation attains the Landauer floor.
\item
  \textbf{The income flux and the Master Solvency Condition.} With
  \(\beta=(k_BT)^{-1}\), the external free-energy income satisfies, at
  every time, \[
  \mathrm{Income}(t)\;\ge\;\dot W_{\mathrm{maint}}(t)+\dot W_{\mathrm{damage}}(t)+\dot W_{\mathrm{admin}}(t)+\dot W_{\mathrm{adv,sub}}(t)+\dot W_{\mathrm{hold}}(t),
  \] the five rate components being: boundary maintenance
  \(\omega_0R(t)^{d_G-1}\); the Landauer-charged damage-repair demand
  \(k_BT\,\dot{\mathcal D}_{\mathrm{damage}}\) including the
  tracking-controller export \(k_BT\gamma_{\mathrm{leak}}\); reversible
  administration at the Landauer floor together with emulation overhead;
  adversarial sub-threshold absorption; and the minimal-hold drain that
  quarantine requires. Income originates outside the escrow network and
  inside the one structure (P1); redistribution within the network ---
  the authority-attenuation tax --- is never a source.
\item
  \textbf{Code, sensing, \(\Pi\), and the twenty-one charged accounts.}
  A stabilizer code of distance \(d\) covering the physical error
  channels; QND syndrome sampling with the declared latency; per-site
  sensor power threshold \(\varepsilon_{\mathrm{sens}}\) and the
  quantum-speed-limit rate bound; the embedding \(\Pi\) of the logical
  boundary fragments into projection operators; and the twenty-one
  charged fault accounts with genuine conditional kernels, whose sum is
  bounded by \(\sum\delta\le10^{-6}\) per declared horizon.
\end{enumerate}

\textbf{4.1 status of the stack} (Section 17; compiled counterparts in
Section 23):

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  Premise 1 is the frame and stays given.
\item
  Premise 2 is reduced to its gate-set clause by the Lie--Trotter
  product formula and Lloyd's simulation theorem
  (\texttt{T\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n}).
\item
  Premise 3 remains standard, with P-mon derived within it.
\item
  Premise 4 is derived from P1/P4.
\item
  The full premise-5 target remains reversible administration. The canon
  proves the heat-only option-model result and, for the implemented
  Bennett program with its complete lifecycle cost, the exact condition:
  reversible administration is forced inside the adiabatic window
  \(0<Q-H\), \(A/(Q-H)<\tau_{\max}\), and outside that window the
  counterpart is not cheaper
  (\texttt{B\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}E\allowbreak{}x\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}5\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}x\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t},
  \texttt{window\_exact};
  \texttt{P\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}F\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d};
  G.17). The remaining physical step is to show that the installation's
  actual heat, lifecycle charges and deadline lie inside the window.
\item
  The income ledger has a necessary-for-relevance resource split
  (\texttt{P\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}F\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e}).
  Whole-outcome greatestness uses both realisability and funded cost
  (Part II.10); G2 retains the full construction and Section 22 states
  the installation ledger.
\item
  Premise 7 is a parameter table. Its ceiling is met at some code
  distance below threshold
  (\texttt{T\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g}),
  and its quantum-speed-limit rate bound is derived
  (\texttt{S\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}L\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}A\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e}).
\end{enumerate}

The second law in the form of guard 1 is derived from Landauer's theorem
(\texttt{Q\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}L\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}q\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e}).
The premises still carried are listed exactly in §23.8.

\subsection{I.3 Evidence conventions}\label{i.3-evidence-conventions}

Three kinds of evidence are distinguished and never conflated.
\textbf{Compiled components} are cited by theorem and receipt --- the
published core, DOI 10.5281/zenodo.23599528, carries six Lean modules
with compiled artifacts. \textbf{Exact replay receipts} --- the review
banks' finite checks and the hash-chained traces of the replayable
four-stroke ledger --- certify the instances and identities they compute
and nothing more. \textbf{Prose derivations} hold at their stated
premises. A prose proof does not acquire a compiler certificate by
association. Notation: entropies in natural logarithms unless a section
states bits; \(D(\rho\|\tau)\) against the constrained reference on the
protected subspace, never the ambient Gibbs state; the four-stage order
\(\preceq\) of the law, extended by the resource stage of Part III,
Chapter 5.

\textbf{4.1.} The machine-checked tier now includes \textbf{the
machine-checked canon of this edition}: one hundred and fifteen Lean
modules compiled against Mathlib rev \texttt{56a005855ea1}, each with
exit 0, zero compiler output and \texttt{leanchecker} passing, with no
\texttt{sorry} and only Lean's standard axioms. Section 23 maps each
compiled result to its module and theorem, and states what each rests
on. The conventions above are unchanged: a result is called compiled
only where Section 23 maps it.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{Part II --- The law, the physical holding bridge, and the
retained
instruments}\label{part-ii-the-law-the-physical-holding-bridge-and-the-retained-instruments}

This part supplies the argument in this edition. Its scope is every
particular and every relation, including nonliving particulars and
hostile minds. The full Universal Protection Theory remains the
proposition to be demonstrated. The constructions below strengthen its
proof chain; none substitutes a smaller protected domain or a weaker
outcome for that proposition.

\subsection{II.1 The preservation order and why its scope is
all}\label{ii.1-the-preservation-order-and-why-its-scope-is-all}

At a history, let \(E\) be the existing particulars and \(N\) those not
yet made. An option is a complete policy or trajectory with outcome

\[
\mathcal O(o)=(L_E(o),F_E(o),L_N(o),F_N(o)),
\qquad F_E(o)\subseteq L_E(o),\quad F_N(o)\subseteq L_N(o).
\]

The \(L\) sets record continuation and the \(F\) sets continuation in
the particular's own direction. A generated particular joins the
existing domain at the next history in which it exists. Particulars
retain their identities in this comparison: gains are compared by set
inclusion, not interchangeable headcounts.

Write \(o\prec q\) when the first unequal stage is a strict inclusion in
\(q\)'s favour, with all earlier stages equal. Equality of all four
stages or this strict relation gives \(o\preceq q\). Transitivity
follows by taking the earlier of the first strict stages in two
comparisons; if the stages coincide, strict inclusion is transitive. An
aligned option is maximal in the actual feasible family. A greatest
option dominates every member of that family. The distinction matters
when feasible options are incomparable.

\textbf{Proposition 1 --- no needless loss.} A needless loss at stage
\(j\) has a feasible alternative agreeing at every earlier stage and
strictly including the chosen stage-\(j\) set. Such an alternative is
exactly a strict improvement in the canonical order. Therefore alignment
is equivalent to no needless loss at any stage, without a finiteness
assumption. This is
\texttt{G\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}s}.

\textbf{Proposition 2 --- finite existence and the life guard.} In a
finite nonempty feasible family, repeatedly taking a strict improvement
terminates: transitivity and irreflexivity prevent repetition. For a
feasible standby \(b\), restrict first to the nonempty family
\(\{o:L_E(b)\subseteq L_E(o)\}\). A maximal member \(g\) of this family
is maximal in the whole family, since any improvement of \(g\) still
keeps everyone standby keeps and would belong to the restricted family.
Thus an aligned option can retain everyone standby retains
(\texttt{L\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}x\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d},
\texttt{life\_guard}). Infinite-family existence uses its own
chain-bound or compactness argument, rather than the finite proof.

\textbf{Proposition 3 --- full scope is forced.} Suppose maximisation on
a scope \(S\) guarantees no needless existing loss for every feasible
family. If \(x\notin S\), consider the family \(\{\varnothing,\{x\}\}\).
Its two outcomes have the same projection onto \(S\), so the empty
outcome is projected-maximal, yet needlessly loses \(x\). This
contradiction gives \(S\) equal to the whole domain. Conversely, with
full scope a strict existing improvement is visible to the maximiser.
Counting a new particular at the same priority as an existing one also
fails: keeping \(x\) and instead making \(y\) can become incomparable,
permitting the avoidable loss of \(x\). This proves the full-scope
criterion and the separation of existing and new priorities
(\texttt{L\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}f},
\texttt{s\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}s}).

The holding construction uses this result directly.
\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}w}
derives \(\forall x, x\in S\) from the law's guarantee over feasible
families. Coverage is therefore an output of the law argument, rather
than a new assumption that only agreeable minds count.

\subsection{II.2 Reasons or faithful holding, including the ruthless
optimiser}\label{ii.2-reasons-or-faithful-holding-including-the-ruthless-optimiser}

For each mind \(m\), the bridge has the form \(R(m)\lor H(m)\). Sections
15--16 develop the mind's own reasons \(R(m)\); the holding construction
supplies \(H(m)\), including when persuasion does not succeed. The
bounded indifferent defector remains within the proof's scope. Its
continued care and the restraint of its harmful effects do not depend on
converting its terminal objective.

\textbf{Proposition 4 --- faithful-hold dominance.} Suppose a hold \(h\)
is statewise at least as good as a rival \(\pi\) in the complete outcome
order: \(\mathcal O(\pi,w)\preceq\mathcal O(h,w)\) for each world \(w\).
For an upward-closed event \(U\), its indicators then satisfy

\[
1_U(\mathcal O(\pi,w))\le 1_U(\mathcal O(h,w)).
\]

Integration preserves this inequality under any probability law on
worlds. If the hold keeps all existing particulars and the rival loses
one with positive probability, the event that all existing particulars
continue is strictly more probable under the hold
(\texttt{L\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s},
\texttt{f\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t}).
The premise concerns the entire outcome tuple, including freedoms and
authorised opportunities; the continuation mechanism in II.3 supplies
the physical continuation component of that full argument.

Restraint removes a harmful command, authority or effect channel while
continuing the particular's care.
\texttt{H\allowbreak{}5\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e}
records this invariant. A pure optimiser's internal refusal does not
disable application-time checks, consume its maintenance allocation as
punishment, or remove it from the existing domain. Sections 15--16 and
20.4 therefore work together: reasons where the objective supplies them,
and faithful holding throughout the protected scope.

\subsection{II.3 The holding cycle is a
construction}\label{ii.3-the-holding-cycle-is-a-construction}

Let a maintained record \(c_x\) for particular \(x\) be a codeword in a
finite nonempty code \(C\), with distinct codewords separated by at
least \(d_{\min}\). Choose a decoding radius \(r_{\mathrm{dec}}\) with
\(2r_{\mathrm{dec}}<d_{\min}\). Finiteness supplies a nearest-codeword
decoder
(\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}x\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r}).

\textbf{Unique decoding.} If \(d(y,c_x)\le r_{\mathrm{dec}}\), the
nearest-codeword property gives
\(d(y,\operatorname{dec}(y))\le r_{\mathrm{dec}}\). A different decoded
codeword would satisfy

\[
d_{\min}\le d(\operatorname{dec}(y),c_x)
\le d(\operatorname{dec}(y),y)+d(y,c_x)
\le2r_{\mathrm{dec}}<d_{\min},
\]

a contradiction. Thus decoding returns the actual maintained record
(\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s}).
The conclusion identifies that record, rather than merely distinguishing
two arbitrary output states.

Define continuation at resolution \(\varepsilon\) by
\(d(a_x,c_x)\le\varepsilon\). One holding cycle consists of the world's
perturbation, decoding, and funded repair. If the perturbation remains
within \(r_{\mathrm{dec}}\), unique decoding returns \(c_x\); if repair
prepares the decoded record to precision
\(\varepsilon_f\le\varepsilon\), then the repaired state continues. This
is
\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s}.
The perturbation includes the environment and any adversarial digital
mind.

The decoding-radius obligation has physical derivations. For continuous
perturbation, let the reference be stationary in the chosen record frame
under \(H_0\), and let the actual trajectory solve Schrödinger dynamics
under \(H_0+V\), with self-adjoint generators and \(\|V(s)\|\le W\). The
speed-limit comparison gives

\[
\|\psi(s_2)-c_x\|\le\|\psi(s_1)-c_x\|+\frac W\hbar(s_2-s_1).
\]

Consequently, if \(\varepsilon+(W/\hbar)\Delta\le r_{\mathrm{dec}}\),
every time in the interval of length \(\Delta\) stays within the
decoding radius
(\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}q\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}h},
\texttt{q\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}s}).
The bound includes the interval between correction steps. Its compiled
norm is state-vector norm; a density-matrix trace-distance application
supplies the corresponding norm conversion, rather than identifying the
two norms.

For discrete faults, let the number accumulated before response be
\(N\le\lambda\tau\). With \(2\lambda\tau<d\), integer headroom gives
\(N\le\lfloor(d-1)/2\rfloor\), inside the unique-decoding radius
(\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}s},
\texttt{E\allowbreak{}9\allowbreak{}S\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}H\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m}).

\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l}
joins these pieces for a state with a discrete error frame and a
continuous state vector. Correction returns the frame to its record,
refresh returns the continuous state to precision \(\varepsilon_f\), and
throughout the interval

\[
\|\psi(s)-c_x\|\le\varepsilon+(W/\hbar)\Delta.
\]

No premise in this cycle asks the perturbing agent to want preservation.
Its source, event, observation and fault-account construction for the
full physical domain is G1, constructed in G.10
(\texttt{G\allowbreak{}1\allowbreak{}H\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n},
\texttt{G\allowbreak{}1\allowbreak{}E\allowbreak{}9\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}9}).
The existing cycle and its derivations remain incorporated while that
construction is supplied.

\textbf{Connection to the supplied decoder interface.}
\texttt{D\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}R\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}y}
constructs the supplied \texttt{DecoderPair} from this existing finite
nearest-codeword theorem. Encoding is the inclusion of the maintained
code; decoding returns a codeword. The nearest-point inequality at a
clean record has distance zero, so decoding after encoding is the
identity. The same constructed pair corrects every received word within
the radius above. The retraction and its noisy-input guarantee are
derived together.

\textbf{A non-vanishing maintained witness.} If the physical null state
is represented by zero and \(\varepsilon<d(c_x,0)\), continuation
implies \(a_x\ne0\): otherwise \(d(a_x,c_x)=d(c_x,0)>\varepsilon\). This
is
\texttt{D\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}R\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n}.
The initial record, its separation from null, the funded controller and
the source refinement supply this operational persistence. The full
scope of particulars remains the scope established in II.1.

\subsection{II.4 Tracking follows from the
controller}\label{ii.4-tracking-follows-from-the-controller}

For a fixed record \(r\), the reset controller \(\dot x=-\kappa(x-r)\)
has the exact solution

\[
x(t)=r+e^{-\kappa t}(x(0)-r).
\]

Differentiation verifies the equation. Over a step \(\Delta\), put
\(\alpha=1-e^{-\kappa\Delta}\); the update is \((1-\alpha)x+\alpha r\),
whose distance to \(r\) is exactly \((1-\alpha)\|x-r\|\). These are
\texttt{I\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}w},
\texttt{lindblad\_step} and \texttt{reset\_contracts}.

For moving records, suppose the actual update and combined
injection/record drift are

\[
x_{k+1}=(1-\alpha)(x_k+\xi_k)+\alpha r_{k+1},
\qquad\|\xi_k\|+\|r_{k+1}-r_k\|\le b,
\quad0<\alpha\le1.
\]

Subtract \(r_{k+1}\), use exact contraction and the triangle inequality,
and use \(1-\alpha\le1\). The error \(e_k=\|x_k-r_k\|\) obeys

\[
e_{k+1}\le(1-\alpha)e_k+b,
\qquad
e_k\le(1-\alpha)^k e_0+\frac b\alpha\bigl(1-(1-\alpha)^k\bigr).
\]

The second inequality follows by induction and a geometric sum. Thus the
tracking envelope is derived from the update, rather than independently
postulated
(\texttt{I\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d},
\texttt{t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d}).
When a rate-discretised update uses
\(\alpha=(\Gamma_{\mathrm{corr}}+\kappa_{\mathrm{ctrl}})\Delta t\le1\)
and \(b=\gamma_{\mathrm{leak}}\Delta t+\varepsilon_{\mathrm{fault}}\),
its limiting floor is

\[
\frac b\alpha=
\frac{\gamma_{\mathrm{leak}}+\varepsilon_{\mathrm{fault}}/\Delta t}
{\Gamma_{\mathrm{corr}}+\kappa_{\mathrm{ctrl}}}.
\]

The exact exponential reset and this rate-discretised update have their
stated coefficients. Leakage remains in the injection, and its
controller export remains in the damage-repair account. Sections 18 and
22 fund the controller; contraction supplies no free energy.

\subsection{II.5 The complete attack split and continuing
care}\label{ii.5-the-complete-attack-split-and-continuing-care}

E9 uses all three branches. Below the per-site threshold, the speed
limit bounds injection and the funded tracking/refresh mechanism absorbs
it (Branch A). A non-stabilizer Pauli error of weight below the code
distance has a nonzero syndrome, so sampling detects it (Branch B). A
logical operator invisible to the syndrome and not a stabilizer has
support of at least \(d\) sites (Branch C).

For that support there is an exhaustive alternative: either every site's
delivery is below threshold, returning to Branch A, or at least one site
reaches threshold. At such a site, the honest-sensor response places
every honest reading above the alarm level. A robust quorum estimate
then exceeds that level despite up to \(f\) faulty sensors
(\texttt{E\allowbreak{}9\allowbreak{}S\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}H\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g}).
This is
\texttt{I\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e}.
The proof requires neither that all support sites cross threshold
together nor an independent attack at each site. The honest-sensor
response, sampling latency and timely service belong to the physical
certificate.

The response budget joins detection, decision and repair before
irreversible harm, and buys the code headroom used in II.3. The
controller's authorised effects pass the application-time fence. In the
source register, the actual target and payload, semantic epoch,
generation floor, authority, revocation, expiry, remaining credit and
nonce are checked against finalised grants. Funding is charged once;
replay supplies no second effect. Admission-time checking alone would
leave an old queued effect able to land after revocation, which is why
the check belongs at application.

The nine A1 channels are memory mapping, DMA, context switches, packet
ingestion, bus messages, storage synchronisation, commissioning,
delegation and retirement. Each has a refinement carrier and enforcement
obligation. Timely care also needs a live legitimate effect or a timely
certified alternative; safely refusing a stale permit proves refusal,
while
\texttt{S\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}F\allowbreak{}I\allowbreak{}F\allowbreak{}O\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e}
in \texttt{R17FIFOR17A.lean} supplies the successful delivery result for
its source construction. Continuing care remains invariant during
restraint.

\subsection{II.6 Reactive refinement and one joint failure
bound}\label{ii.6-reactive-refinement-and-one-joint-failure-bound}

\textbf{Proposition 5 --- reactive lockstep.} Let \(T\) be the source
transition, \(U\) the abstract transition and \(p\) their projection.
Suppose

\[
p(T(s,c,e))=U(p(s),c,e),
\qquad\operatorname{obs}_S(s)=\operatorname{obs}_A(p(s)).
\]

Initially equal histories choose the same controller command. An
environment reacting to that history and command chooses the same
response. The commuting equation then gives equal next projected states,
and observation agreement extends equal histories. Induction proves
equality at every finite step. If the environment observes the
post-command result, the command and response phases commute separately
and agree at the intermediate observation
(\texttt{C\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}p},
\texttt{r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}₂}).
Pending effects remain in the causal graph and its closed components or
directed separators.

\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d}
composes the law-derived coverage, the holding step and this refinement.
For each \(\omega\in G\), its input \texttt{hcert} contains five
components: commuting refinement, observation agreement, the initial
continuation witness for every particular, the perturbation-radius
bound, and repair precision. The first three initialise and transfer the
source execution; the last two supply
\texttt{h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s}
at every step. Induction therefore proves

\[
\forall\omega\in G,\ \forall k\le T,\ \forall x,
\quad\operatorname{Continues}(p(s_k(\omega)),x).
\]

This is stronger than stopping at the abstract \(R\lor H\) interface:
the code, decoder, repair and coverage derivations are in the
composition. G.10 proves the five \texttt{hcert} components on the held
source. The full-domain G1 target additionally requires the actual
installation/source correspondence and response device.

For risk, use actual named source events \(E_i\), with conditional
kernels bounded by \(\delta_i\) at every admitted history, and establish
\(G^c\subseteq\bigcup_i E_i\). The tower property gives unconditional
event bounds and the union bound gives

\[
P(\exists k\le T,\exists x:\neg\operatorname{Continues}(s_k,x))
\le P(G^c)\le\sum_i\delta_i.
\]

No independence is used.
\texttt{C\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}8\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n},
\texttt{T\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}L\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t}
and
\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d}
implement this assembly. Source bindings such as \texttt{R14Boundary}
and \texttt{R16R14} supply concrete safe transitions, named exceptions
and projections; their role is to build the certificate, with external
weather, replica faults and recruitment obligations kept distinct.

\subsection{II.7 Complete workload and timely authorised
effects}\label{ii.7-complete-workload-and-timely-authorised-effects}

\textbf{Proposition 6 --- FIFO completion.} For a nonpreemptive FIFO
lane with releases \(r_i\), complete demand bounds \(b_i\) and inherited
bound \(F_0\), define \(F_i=\max(r_i,F_{i-1})+b_i\). If actual demand
\(a_i\le b_i\), actual inherited completion \(C_0\le F_0\), and all
blocking and idle delay are accounted for, then

\[
C_i\le\max(r_i,C_{i-1})+a_i
\le\max(r_i,F_{i-1})+b_i=F_i.
\]

Induction proves every admitted deadline \(d_i\) with \(F_i\le d_i\).
Jointly attainable upper demands attain equality, giving tightness
(\texttt{W\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e},
\texttt{fifo\_deadlines}, \texttt{fifo\_tight}). For serial stages the
double induction uses
\(U_{i,k}=\max(U_{i,k-1},U_{i-1,k})+\tau_k(b_{i,k})\)
(\texttt{s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e}).
Each stage denotes a real available server or a correctly shared
schedule. Carry-in, verification, journals, receipts and final
application remain in the demand vector.

The retained capacity rows are \(17=6+11\) and \(22=6+16\), with batch
size 3 in their stated certificate family. The exact pool bill is
\(48000\cdot17+67200\cdot22=2{,}294{,}400\). These are total capacities.
Five-of-seven quorum intersection gives safety with at most two
Byzantine identities; no-pause progress additionally buys the timely
proposal, communication, validation, durable commit, receipts and
recovery path within the continuity margin.

\subsection{II.8 Paid generation, deduction and
reflection}\label{ii.8-paid-generation-deduction-and-reflection}

Generation reserves the complete continuing obligation before
activation. Delegation strictly attenuates authority; retirement
releases capacity only after activity and outstanding obligations end.
The H5 transition invariant yields reserve-before-activation, zero debt,
no unfunded work and preservation of care (\texttt{H5Closure}).

For deduction boundaries spanned by \(A\) and protected anchors spanned
by \(G_A\),

\[
\operatorname{col}(A)\cap\operatorname{col}(G_A)=\{0\}
\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
\operatorname{rank}[A\ G_A]=\operatorname{rank}A+\operatorname{rank}G_A.
\]

\textbf{Proposition 7 --- context-closed finite certificates.} If
primitive boundaries lie in \(\operatorname{im}A\) and every generating
context satisfies \(C_jA=AM_j\), then
\(C_j(Av)=A(M_jv)\in\operatorname{im}A\). The image is closed under
sums. Structural induction on a finite deduction built from these
contexts and sums therefore keeps every boundary in that image
(\texttt{D\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}B\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n},
\texttt{b\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e}).
Section 20 gives the projection/meet interpretation, with the actual
anchor-preserving encoding.

For reflection, a contraction of constant \(0<k<1\) on a complete metric
space has a unique fixed point. If \(s_{n+1}=R(s_n)\), later increments
are bounded by the geometric tail of the last increment, giving

\[
d(s_{n+1},s^*)\le\frac{k}{1-k}d(s_n,s_{n+1}),
\qquad d(s_n,s^*)\le\frac{k^n a_0}{1-k},\quad a_0=d(s_0,s_1).
\]

For \(a_0>0\), a sufficient paid update count is
\(\max\{1,\lceil\ln(\epsilon(1-k)/a_0)/\ln k\rceil\}\), using
\(\ln k<0\). Reserve the complete update, observation and stopping-test
costs. A zero initial increment is already fixed; a zero contraction
constant reaches the fixed point on its first update
(\texttt{R\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e},
\texttt{apriori\_error}, \texttt{stopping\_count},
\texttt{z\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}x\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d}).

\subsection{II.9 Conserved allocation and the charged
ledger}\label{ii.9-conserved-allocation-and-the-charged-ledger}

In the conserved routing family, demand rows \(D_{ij}\ge0\) sum to one
and shares \(s_i\ge0\) sum to one; routed flow is \(s_iD_{ij}\). With
regular demand-weighted cost \(m_i=m\) and local cost \(\alpha\ge0\),

\[
\Psi(s)=(m+\alpha)\operatorname{HHI}(s),\qquad
\Psi_{\min}=\frac{m+\alpha}{n},
\] \[
\Psi(s)=\Psi_{\min}\left(1+(n-1)\frac{\operatorname{HHI}(s)-1/n}{1-1/n}\right)
\quad(n>1).
\]

The parenthesis simplifies to \(n\operatorname{HHI}\). Since
\(\sum_i(s_i-1/n)^2=\operatorname{HHI}-1/n\), uniform allocation is the
unique minimum when \(m+\alpha>0\). These are identities for the stated
conserved-routing family. The revised JW2.6 charged-account arithmetic
is approximately \(2.06419526094\times10^{-7}\), within the \(10^{-6}\)
horizon ceiling. Sections 18 and 22 retain the complete entropy, income
and fault ledger.

\subsection{II.10 Earned greatestness and solvent
reciprocity}\label{ii.10-earned-greatestness-and-solvent-reciprocity}

Let \(R\) be the realisable full outcome tuples, \(c\) their complete
cost, and \(F_B=\{o\in R:c(o)\le B\}\). Let \(t=\top\) mean the greatest
\textbf{whole} tuple, with the continuation and freedom stages retained.
Two different constructions enter: \texttt{htopR} establishes
\(t\in R\), and \texttt{hsolv} establishes \(c(t)\le B\). Together they
put \(t\) in \(F_B\).

For any aligned \(o\in F_B\), componentwise \(o\le t\). If \(o\ne t\),
the first unequal stage makes \(t\) a strict feasible improvement,
contradicting alignment. Hence \(o=t\), and every rival tuple
\(q\le o\), with equality or strict canonical inferiority
(\texttt{U\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}q\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t},
\texttt{g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t},
\texttt{s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t}).
This proof needs no union-closure premise. It earns the identification
of the aligned option with the greatest tuple after the two
constructions; budget accounting supplies affordability, while the
whole-tuple realisability construction requested in \textbf{G2} supplies
\texttt{htopR}.

With the semantic reading \texttt{htopkeeps} that this top continues
every existing particular,
\texttt{S\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}R\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e}
keeps every mind, and
\texttt{r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t}
keeps any mind any rival keeps, without a rival-feasibility or
union-closure hypothesis on that branch. The full freedom stages remain
part of greatestness.

Income-or-irrelevance supplies the resource split: either the ledger
funds every step or it has a first negative balance
(\texttt{P\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}F\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e},
\texttt{forced\_breach}).
\texttt{U\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t}
applies the same whole-tuple realisability construction at each step.
Its negative branch identifies a regime that has failed protection; it
does not protect someone by renaming failure. The funded holding, timing
and mediation construction is what prevents the relevant breach. The
finite-slack/affordable-union theorems remain available for their
resource cases without replacing the universal target.

The X3 information, carrier, traffic and work proofs are given in full
in Section 21 of this edition. The three joined supports G1--G3 are
constructed in G.9--G.12 with the maximal targets unchanged.

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\section{Part III --- The canon}\label{part-iii-the-canon}

\subsection{Chapter 1 (§8). The four-stroke thermodynamic
core}\label{chapter-1-8.-the-four-stroke-thermodynamic-core}

\textbf{1.1 The corrected stroke identity.} With the constrained
reference \(\tau\), fixed endpoint Hamiltonian, work \(W_{\mathrm{on}}\)
and exported heat \(Q_B\), and correlation change \(I_1\to I_2\):

\[
\Delta D_{S,\mathrm{II}}=-\Delta S_S+\beta\,\Delta U_S=\beta W_{\mathrm{on}}-\beta Q_B+I_1-I_2,
\qquad
\Delta D_{S,\mathrm{II}}=\beta W_{\mathrm{on}}-\beta Q_B+I_M
\]

when the syndrome correlation is fully consumed. Consuming correlation
contributes \textbf{\(+I_M\)} at non-negative work; information caps the
increase (Sagawa--Ueda) and never supplies a free decrease.

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Integration guard 1.} The operative export bound is the
unconditional second-law form
\(\boxed{\beta Q_B\ge I_M+D_{\mathrm{err}}}\) --- the bath must absorb
the exported entropy. The condensed form
\(Q_B\ge W_{\mathrm{on}}+I_M+D_{\mathrm{err}}\) is regime-dependent (it
reduces to \(D_{\mathrm{err}}\ge\sigma_{\mathrm{irr}}\) under a
purifying repair and fails when intrinsic dissipation exceeds damage)
and appears only as a noted special case.
\end{quote}

\textbf{4.1.} Guard 1 is a compiled theorem. Landauer's bound holds for
every unitary coupling of a register to a bath in its Gibbs state,
\(S(\rho_A)-S(\rho'_A)\le\beta\,\Delta Q_B\)
(\texttt{Q\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}L\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}q\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r}).
The guard \(\beta Q_B\ge I_M+D_{\mathrm{err}}+\delta_I\) follows
whenever the reset removes that entropy
(\texttt{q\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e}),
and the per-cycle work floor of 1.2 follows with guard 1 derived
(\texttt{q\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r}).
The stroke identity, the funded-repair certificate and the canon
invariant are compiled (\texttt{ThermoLedger}). Section 18 gives the
complete ledger.

\textbf{1.2 The funded-repair certificate.} There are no free repairs:
per cycle,
\(W_{\mathrm{cycle}}\ge k_BT(I_M+D_{\mathrm{err}}+\delta_I)+(1-\eta)C\),
equality only in the reversible limit; the ledger charges gross. With
escrow \(E\), income \(r(t)\), cumulative demand \(\mathcal D(t)\):

\[
E(0)+\int_0^t r\,ds-\mathcal D(t)\ge0\ \text{for all }t\ \Longrightarrow\ \text{protection is maintained},
\]

with within-cycle excursions bounded by the charged kernels, and
insolvency a charged failure event \(\delta_{\mathrm{fund}}\) joining
the twenty-one accounts (tower/union composition; no independence). The
draft series' compound storage functional is retired as a certificate:
under consistent accounting its stroke change is identically
\(-(2D_{\mathrm{err}}+I_M)\), independent of \(C\), \(\eta\), and
\(\sigma\) --- bookkeeping, not funding.

\textbf{1.3 The four strokes.} I --- QND extraction
(\(W_{\mathrm{meas}}\); ancilla entropy \(+I_M+\delta_I\)). II ---
funded repair (\(C\) at \(\eta\); dissipative drive; identity and guard
above). III --- Bennett reset (\(k_BT(I_M+\delta_I)\) from income). IV
--- maintenance and slack (\(\omega_0R^{d_G-1}\); tax redistributes and
conserves; administration at the Landauer floor; residual to the slack
register of Chapter 5).

\textbf{1.4 Ledger evidence.} The sealed, hash-chained, exact-rational
traces of
\texttt{L\allowbreak{}E\allowbreak{}D\allowbreak{}G\allowbreak{}E\allowbreak{}R\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}F\allowbreak{}O\allowbreak{}U\allowbreak{}R\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}S\allowbreak{}T\allowbreak{}R\allowbreak{}O\allowbreak{}K\allowbreak{}E\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}2\allowbreak{}0\allowbreak{}2\allowbreak{}6\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}1\allowbreak{}0\allowbreak{}-\allowbreak{}2\allowbreak{}4\allowbreak{}/}
assert per cycle: \(\Delta D_{\mathrm{II}}=-D_{\mathrm{err}}\),
first-law heat, guard 1, funded reset, tax conservation, floor
administration, bath monotonicity, and reserve-before-activation gating.

\subsection{Chapter 2 (§11). Identity by continuation and the active
tracking
controller}\label{chapter-2-11.-identity-by-continuation-and-the-active-tracking-controller}

Identity is carried by the particular's continuation. Part II.3 derives
the holding cycle: nearest decoding returns the maintained record inside
the unique-decoding radius, and funded repair restores continuation. The
speed limit controls the state at every intermediate time; E9 supplies
discrete code headroom.
\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l}
joins the discrete error frame and continuous state-vector bound without
assuming that the perturbing agent wants protection.

Part II.4 derives tracking from the actual reset update. For a fixed
record \(r\), the exact flow is \(r+e^{-\kappa t}(x_0-r)\). With
\(\alpha=1-e^{-\kappa\Delta}\), injection and moving-record drift
bounded by \(b\), the update yields

\[
e_{k+1}\le(1-\alpha)e_k+b,\qquad
e_k\le(1-\alpha)^ke_0+\frac b\alpha(1-(1-\alpha)^k).
\]

\texttt{I\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}w},
\texttt{lindblad\_step}, \texttt{reset\_contracts},
\texttt{t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d}
and
\texttt{t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d}
prove this chain. For the rate-discretised correction/controller update,
the floor is

\[
e_\infty=\frac{\gamma_{\mathrm{leak}}+\varepsilon_{\mathrm{fault}}/\Delta t}
{\Gamma_{\mathrm{corr}}+\kappa_{\mathrm{ctrl}}}.
\]

Leakage remains in the injection and controller export
\(k_BT\gamma_{\mathrm{leak}}\) remains charged. Section 18 funds this
drain; Section 22 supplies the numerical design ledger. The exact reset
coefficient and the rate-discretised coefficient retain their respective
meanings.

The law forces the full holding scope
(\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}w}).
\texttt{B\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}d}
composes that coverage with the cycle, reactive refinement and joint
fault bound. G1, its full-domain source construction, is proved in G.10.
The universal continuation-and-freedom target remains intact.

\subsection{Chapter 3 (§12). Adversarial containment: the E9
trichotomy}\label{chapter-3-12.-adversarial-containment-the-e9-trichotomy}

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Integration guard 2.} Containment is the full three-branch
trichotomy; no single branch closes it.
\end{quote}

\textbf{Theorem (E9, assembled).} Under the header premises, no
adversarial strategy causes uncompensated state divergence without
detection or charge, and
\(t_{\mathrm{detect}}+t_{\mathrm{decision}}+t_{\mathrm{repair}}<t_{\mathrm{irreversible\ harm}}\)
on the good event.

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{Branch A --- sub-threshold per-site injection}
  (\(P_{\mathrm{adv}}<\varepsilon_{\mathrm{sens}}\)): bounded
  accumulation, absorbed and refreshed (Chapter 2); the attrition
  contest is won whenever income exceeds the capped drain.
\item
  \textbf{Branch B --- low-weight syndrome detection:} any
  non-stabilizer Pauli component of weight \(\le d-1\) anticommutes with
  some generator and is detected within sampling latency, independent of
  direction (correctability to \(\lfloor(d-1)/2\rfloor\); the low-weight
  centralizer contains only identity and stabilizers; logicals have
  weight \(\ge d\)); detection is certain on the non-stabilizer mass.
\item
  \textbf{Branch C --- coherent logical injection} (weight \(\ge d\),
  zero syndrome, not a stabilizer): its support has at least \(d\)
  sites. Either every support-site delivery is below threshold,
  returning to the Branch A rate and refresh bound, or at least one site
  reaches threshold and its honest sensors force a robust-quorum alarm
  despite the faulty reports.
  \texttt{I\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e}
  proves this exhaustive split with the honest-sensor response explicit.
  The proof does not require every support site to cross threshold
  simultaneously.
\item
  \textbf{Latency budget:}
  \(t_{\mathrm{total}}\le a(1+k_{\mathrm{rounds}}d_{\mathrm{local}})/c_{\mathrm{lattice}}+\tau_{\mathrm{sensor}}+\tau_{\mathrm{fb}}+\tau_{\mathrm{reset}}<(r_{\mathrm{critical}}-r_0)/c_{\mathrm{lattice}}\),
  enforced by instance scaling. Residual channels are the charged
  accounts under the ceiling.
\end{itemize}

\textbf{4.1.} The assembled E9 is compiled
(\texttt{E\allowbreak{}9\allowbreak{}S\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}H\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}9\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}g}),
with Branch B's syndrome detection (\texttt{syndrome\_detects}) and the
Byzantine quorum
(\texttt{r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}w\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t},
\texttt{exists\_robust}). Branch A's quantum-speed-limit rate bound is
derived from unitary dynamics: divergence grows at rate at most
\(P_{\mathrm{adv}}/(\hbar\,\omega_{\mathrm{cutoff}})\) under the
per-site coupling bound
(\texttt{S\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}L\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}A\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e}),
with the sharp Mandelstam--Tamm bound
(\texttt{S\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}L\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}m}).
Section 19 gives the full treatment.

\subsection{Chapter 4 (§13). Logical extension: the meet algebra
(H5)}\label{chapter-4-13.-logical-extension-the-meet-algebra-h5}

The declared embedding \(\Pi\) assigns each formula the projection on
its model subspace, with conjunction as the meet
\(P_{\phi\wedge\psi}=\lim_n(P_\phi P_\psi)^n\) (von Neumann); a
presentation is consistent iff its global meet is non-zero. The Gram
determinant is retired on two exact counterexamples
(\(\{\psi,\neg\psi\}\): determinant \(4>0\);
\(\{p,q,\neg(p\wedge q)\}\): determinant \(7>0\) with zero triple meet).
\textbf{Theorem:} deduced extensions preserve the anchor meet
identically
(\(P_{\mathcal T_0}\le P_\phi\Rightarrow P_{\mathcal T_0\cup\{\phi\}}=P_{\mathcal T_0}\));
contradictions collapse it to zero; the state machine commits on
non-zero meet norm and rolls back on zero. In finite dimensions this
decision is operational:
\(\operatorname{col}A\cap\operatorname{col}G_A=\{0\}\) exactly when
\(\operatorname{rank}[A\ G_A]=\operatorname{rank}A+\operatorname{rank}G_A\)
(\texttt{D\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}B\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n}),
a finite rank computation. Asserted consistent extensions may shrink the
anchor meet without rollback --- stated, not hidden. Deduction-boundary
closure continues on the banked rank criterion.

\textbf{4.1.} The meet \(P_{\phi\wedge\psi}=\lim_n(P_\phi P_\psi)^n\) is
a theorem. Von Neumann's alternating projection theorem is compiled in
Hilbert space
(\texttt{C\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}V\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}N\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e}),
and so is Halperin's theorem for whole presentations
(\texttt{C\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}P\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}j\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r}).
Anchor preservation and contradiction detection are compiled in the
Hilbert-space model
(\texttt{M\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}H\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}b\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d},
\texttt{c\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d}).
Section 20 gives the full treatment.

\subsection{Chapter 5 (§14). The canon invariant and universal
greatestness}\label{chapter-5-14.-the-canon-invariant-and-universal-greatestness}

\textbf{5.1 The ratified canon.}

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Uncompensated breach is deterministically impossible on the
certified good event; the good event fails with probability at most
\(\sum\delta\le10^{-6}\) per declared horizon.}
\end{quote}

With its three supporting repairs: quarantine has a minimal drain;
\(G=\{\)state machine executes \(\wedge\) income \(\ge\) minimal drain
\(\wedge\) no charged account fires\(\}\); and \(\sum\delta\) prices the
certificate's conditions failing, never breach given the conditions. The
charged-scenario ledger trace executes this sentence (chain head
\texttt{41143bb2…a2788}).

\textbf{5.2 The fifth stage.} Between architectures tied through the
four outcome stages, compare residual thermodynamic slack
\(E_{\mathrm{slack}}(\pi)=E_{\mathrm{escrow}}(t)-W_{\min}(t)-\int_0^t\mathrm{AdminOverhead}\).
This is the law's own order propagated one step into the resource
dimension via P-mon --- not an ad hoc tie-breaker.

\textbf{5.3 Universal greatestness.} The full target remains, over every
physically implementable rival \(\pi\) and reactive world \(w\),

\[
\mathcal O(\pi,w)\preceq\mathcal O(\pi^*,w),
\]

strict except exact stage-1--5 mirrors. The proof separates the
whole-outcome construction from its funding. Let \(R\) be the realisable
complete outcome tuples and \(c\) their complete cost. The full top
tuple must be constructed in \(R\) (\texttt{htopR}); its cost must fit
the budget (\texttt{hsolv}). These put it in the feasible family. If an
aligned outcome were different, that feasible top would be a strict
improvement at the first differing stage, contradicting alignment.
Therefore the aligned outcome equals the top and contains every rival
outcome
(\texttt{U\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}q\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t},
\texttt{g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t},
\texttt{s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t};
Part II.10).

This solvent proof needs no concurrent-emulation or union-closure
premise. The full continuation and freedom stages remain in
\texttt{htopR}; G2 retains the target of identifying the physical
policy/outcome family with the compiled local-component model.
Income-or-irrelevance supplies the resource dichotomy and charging
barrier, while the funded physical construction supplies protection on
the relevant branch. Section 21 gives X3's complete
information--traffic--carrier--work argument, the accepted
dimension-general scaling and predeployment accounting, with the
matching recovery construction G3 visible.

\begin{quote}
\textbf{Integration guards 3 and 4.} The dominance quantifier carries
the mirror exception. P-slack holds in the Landauer--Bennett form only:
reversible administration attains the Landauer floor, so
\(E_{\mathrm{slack}}(\pi^*)\ge E_{\mathrm{slack}}(\pi)\) for every
rival, equality exactly for reversible mirrors. The
variational-selection route is recorded as considered and rejected.
\end{quote}

\textbf{4.1.} These results are compiled:

\begin{itemize}
\tightlist
\item
  the canon invariant:
  \texttt{T\allowbreak{}h\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}L\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t},
  with \(P(\text{breach})\le P(G^c)\le\sum\delta\) and no independence
  assumed;
\item
  universal greatestness under stage-wise union closure, strict except
  exact mirrors:
  \texttt{G\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}d},
  \texttt{s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}x\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s};
\item
  the assembled Universal Protection Theorem:
  \texttt{U\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}P\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n};
\item
  whole-outcome greatestness:
  \texttt{U\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}C\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}u\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}f\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t}
  uses \texttt{htopR} and \texttt{hsolv} to force every aligned outcome
  to equal the full top tuple, without union closure.
  \texttt{g\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t}
  retains the realisability input at every step while deriving the
  solvent-or-first-breach resource split. Part II.10 gives the proof; G2
  retains the full physical policy/outcome correspondence as its
  construction target.
\end{itemize}

The Landauer floor on which P-slack rests is derived (guard 1).

\textbf{5.4 The three powers.} \textbf{P-mon} --- derived at the
standard cost model (budget-gated feasibility with free disposal).
\textbf{P-emu(c)} --- derived from CTD, the shared action interface
(P1/P4), and funded overhead: concurrent emulation of any finite rival
set within slack, realising the stage-wise union; plain single emulation
admits incomparable maximals and does not terminate greatestness ---
concurrency closes it. \textbf{P-slack} --- derived from
Landauer--Bennett given the reversible-administration commitment. Each
is a theorem one level deeper than the draft series declared; none is
derived from nothing. Per Gödel and Tarski the stack bottoms out at Part
I's seven premises --- standard physics and the relational frame --- and
this edition says exactly that.

\textbf{4.2 integration.} The solvent proof uses the realisable whole
top and its funded cost. The shared action interface remains the
interface for real effects. The affordable-union results retain their
resource cases.
\texttt{S\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}R\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}.\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}k\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}e}
and
\texttt{r\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}p\allowbreak{}r\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}t\allowbreak{}y\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}d\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}m\allowbreak{}i\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}a\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}c\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}\_\allowbreak{}s\allowbreak{}o\allowbreak{}l\allowbreak{}v\allowbreak{}e\allowbreak{}n\allowbreak{}t}
already supply the solvent reciprocity restatement, with
\texttt{htopkeeps} stating its continuation semantics.
